# Krishi Bazar environment variables.
# Local dev and automated tests need NOTHING: with DATABASE_URL unset the app runs on embedded
# PGlite (see docs/adr/0004-neon-pglite-seam.md) and photo storage falls back to a local directory.

# Production/preview only: Neon Postgres pooled connection string (Vercel Marketplace or Neon dashboard).
# DATABASE_URL=

# Production/preview only: Vercel Blob store token (create a Blob store in the Vercel dashboard).
# BLOB_READ_WRITE_TOKEN=

@@@FILE .github/workflows/ci.yml@@@
# CI for Krishi Bazar. Versions come from .jules/deps.csv (researched 2026-10-22), not from memory.
# Design: fast (<10 min), deterministic, pinned, one job per concern, readable logs — the Jules CI
# Auto-Fixer works from these logs, so failing output must say what broke.
# Bootstrap rule: Bun jobs run only once package.json exists (the pack lands on main before T01
# scaffolds the app) — `hashFiles` guards keep the initial push green instead of falsely red.
# Notes: guard jobs use the python3 preinstalled on ubuntu-latest (scripts are stdlib-only, 3.9+).
# Lighthouse enforcement flips on when scripts/lighthouse/enforce exists (added by T19 once budgets
# pass) — the workflow itself is never edited after T01.
name: ci
on:
  pull_request:
  push:
    branches: [main]

concurrency:
  group: ci-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  verify:
    if: hashFiles('package.json') != ''
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 15
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7.0.1
      - uses: oven-sh/setup-bun@v2.2.0
        with:
          bun-version: 1.4.3
      - run: bun install --frozen-lockfile
      - name: Types (svelte-check, strict)
        run: bun run check
      - name: Lint (eslint)
        run: bun run lint
      - name: Format (prettier check)
        run: bun run format
      - name: Unit + integration tests with coverage gates (vitest, PGlite)
        run: bun run test:unit
      - name: Build (adapter-vercel, bun1.x)
        run: bun run build
      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v7.0.2
        if: always()
        with:
          name: coverage
          path: coverage/
          retention-days: 7

  e2e:
    if: hashFiles('package.json') != ''
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 15
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7.0.1
      - uses: oven-sh/setup-bun@v2.2.0
        with:
          bun-version: 1.4.3
      - run: bun install --frozen-lockfile
      - run: bunx playwright install --with-deps chromium
      - name: E2E (playwright + axe, PGlite, local photo storage)
        run: bun run test:e2e
      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v7.0.2
        if: always()
        with:
          name: playwright-report
          path: playwright-report/
          retention-days: 7

  lighthouse:
    if: hashFiles('package.json') != ''
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 15
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7.0.1
      - uses: oven-sh/setup-bun@v2.2.0
        with:
          bun-version: 1.4.3
      - run: bun install --frozen-lockfile
      - run: bun run build
      - name: Lighthouse budgets (Perf>=90 A11y>=95 BP>=95 SEO>=95)
        run: bun run lh
      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v7.0.2
        if: always()
        with:
          name: lighthouse
          path: .lighthouse/
          retention-days: 7

  audit:
    if: hashFiles('package.json') != ''
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 5
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7.0.1
      - uses: oven-sh/setup-bun@v2.2.0
        with:
          bun-version: 1.4.3
      - run: bun install --frozen-lockfile
      - name: Dependency audit (fail on High/Critical)
        run: |
          set -o pipefail
          bun audit 2>&1 | tee audit.log || true
          if grep -Eq '\((High|Critical)\)' audit.log; then
            echo "High/Critical advisories found:"; grep -E '\((High|Critical)\)' audit.log; exit 1
          fi

  commitlint:
    if: github.event_name == 'pull_request' && hashFiles('package.json') != ''
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 5
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7.0.1
        with:
          fetch-depth: 0
      - uses: oven-sh/setup-bun@v2.2.0
        with:
          bun-version: 1.4.3
      - run: bun install --frozen-lockfile
      - name: Conventional commits
        run: bunx --bun commitlint --from=origin/${{ github.base_ref }} --to=HEAD --verbose

  ownership-guard:
    if: github.event_name == 'pull_request'
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 3
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7.0.1
        with:
          fetch-depth: 0
      - name: Fail if the PR touches files its task does not own
        env:
          PR_TITLE: ${{ github.event.pull_request.title }}
          PR_HEAD_REF: ${{ github.head_ref }}
          BASE_REF: origin/${{ github.base_ref }}
        run: python3 .jules/tools/ownership_guard.py

  slop-guard:
    if: github.event_name == 'pull_request'
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 3
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7.0.1
        with:
          fetch-depth: 0
      - name: Fail on TODOs, stubs, skipped tests, suppressions (strict - greenfield)
        env:
          BASE_REF: origin/${{ github.base_ref }}
        run: python3 .jules/tools/slop_guard.py --strict
@@@END@@@

@@@FILE .jules/plan.md@@@
# Plan: Krishi Bazar v2 — sequential Jules build (strict profile)

Architect-owned. Repo `Rhythm2310/krishi-bazar` (empty at planning time, default branch `main`).
Execution: **one agent per task, strictly sequential** (ADR-0003) — 21 waves of exactly one task,
chained by dependency edges. Profile **strict**: kit on, `slop_guard --strict`, a review task after
every integration point, re-review after any fix wave. Jules Pro tier (100 daily tasks — the plan
uses 21 + headroom for fix waves). All models `default`.

## Architecture (summary — full decisions in `.jules/spec.md` + `docs/adr/`)
SvelteKit 3 + Svelte 5 + TypeScript strict on Bun 1.4.3; Vercel Functions on the Bun runtime
(beta, one-line nodejs24.x fallback — ADR-0001), region bom1. Neon Postgres free tier in
production; PGlite for dev/tests behind one Drizzle seam (ADR-0004). Money = integer paisa
(ADR-0002). Pure domain core (`src/lib/domain/**`) behind seam S1; server modules (`src/lib/server/**`)
behind S2; Playwright+axe browser journeys behind S3. Frozen researched data contracts:
`src/lib/data/taxonomy.ts` (9 groups, ~100 Bangladesh-specific categories with real-world
freshness windows, seasons, unit conversions, reference prices sourced from DAM 2026-10 /
documented estimates) and `districts.ts` (64 districts). Hand-written token-driven CSS, bn/en
typed dictionaries with instant cookie toggle, dark mode. WebP-only photos direct-to-Blob with a
local storage adapter for dev/CI. Fair-price advisory with absolute farmer override. Order state
machine নতুন→গৃহীত→পথে→সম্পন্ন + বাতিল with atomic stock reservation and lazy 24h expiry.

## Wave diagram (linear)
```
W0  T01 scaffold+toolchain+CI+tracer ─▶ W1  T02 design system+shell+i18n ─▶ W2  T03 data layer
─▶ W3  T04 domain core+market data ─▶ W4  T05 REVIEW IP-1 ─▶ W5  T06 farmer auth
─▶ W6  T07 photo upload ─▶ W7  T08 fair pricing ─▶ W8  T09 create listing ─▶ W9  T10 REVIEW IP-2
─▶ W10 T11 marketplace browse ─▶ W11 T12 detail ─▶ W12 T13 orders+lifecycle ─▶ W13 T14 REVIEW IP-3
─▶ W14 T15 comments ─▶ W15 T16 listing mgmt ─▶ W16 T17 seed+DX ─▶ W17 T18 REVIEW IP-4
─▶ W18 T19 hardening+budgets ─▶ W19 T20 docs+README ─▶ W20 T21 FINAL REVIEW ─▶ fix wave if needed ─▶ re-review
```
Operating loop per wave: launch one session (`swarm.py launch`) → merge its PR when CI is green →
`swarm.py status` → next. After each review task: `swarm.py replan`, paste `replan.md` to the
architect after the word `REPLAN`; clean report → continue; findings → fix wave + re-review.

## Ownership summary (hot files and contracts — full matrix in `.jules/ownership.csv`)
- T01: package.json, bun.lock, all tool configs, vercel.json, CI workflow, app.html, hooks, static/, scripts/lighthouse/, health route.
- T02: styles/tokens (frozen), i18n core (frozen), ui primitives, layout shell, +page.svelte (until T11), +error.svelte (until T19).
- T03: db schema contract (frozen), drizzle migrations, db scripts, districts.ts.
- T04: domain modules (frozen interfaces), taxonomy.ts, rates.ts (frozen), coverage gates.
- Later single-owner surfaces: auth (T06), upload (T07), pricing (T08), listings components+server (T09, deepened T16), market (T11), detail+orders routes (T12→T13→T15 deepen `p/[id]` across waves), orders server (T13), comments (T15), manage+farmer dashboard page (T16), seed (T17), e2e suite+hooks hardening+global gates (T19), docs+license+.env.example (T20).
- Cross-wave deepenings are safe by construction (one task at a time); guards still enforce per-task `owns` on every PR.

## Models
`default` for every task (owner specified none).

## Dependency summary (exact pins + sources in `.jules/deps.csv`, checked 2026-10-22)
Runtime bun 1.4.3 (Vercel `bunVersion 1.4.x`, beta — VERIFY at deploy). Framework: svelte 5.57.2,
kit 3.0.1, adapter-vercel 7.0.0, vite 8.3.4, typescript 6.0.3 (7.x excluded by peer ranges),
svelte-check 4.7.6. Data: drizzle-orm 0.45.4, drizzle-kit 0.31.11, @neondatabase/serverless 1.2.0,
@electric-sql/pglite 0.5.8, zod 4.6.5. Storage: @vercel/blob 2.8.1. UI: @lucide/svelte 1.53.0
(replaces deprecated lucide-svelte), @fontsource/hind-siliguri 5.3.0, @fontsource-variable/inter
5.3.0 — no CSS framework (owner decision). Tests: vitest 5.0.3, @vitest/coverage-v8 5.0.3,
fast-check 4.10.2, @playwright/test 1.64.0, @axe-core/playwright 4.13.0. Lint/format/commits:
eslint 10.12.0, eslint-plugin-svelte 3.23.0, typescript-eslint 8.71.1, @eslint/js 10.0.1,
eslint-config-prettier 10.1.8, globals 17.13.0, prettier 3.9.9, prettier-plugin-svelte 4.1.1,
@commitlint/cli 21.2.3, @commitlint/config-conventional 21.2.3. Perf: lighthouse 13.5.0 (replaces
stale @lhci/cli). Actions: checkout v7.0.1, setup-bun v2.2.0, upload-artifact v7.0.2.

## Risks carried into the build (also in the pack's Assumptions)
Bun runtime beta on Vercel (fallback documented); Neon free-tier cold starts (accepted); Vercel

(documented, one-cell change).

# Paste into Jules: repo page > Configuration > Initial Setup, then click "Run and Snapshot".
# Bun is preinstalled on Jules VMs. Installs are frozen so the snapshot matches .jules/deps.csv.

# dev and tests run fully on PGlite + local photo storage).
set -euo pipefail

bun install --frozen-lockfile
bunx playwright install --with-deps chromium

# Warm-up: proves the toolchain works so every agent starts from a verified base.
bun run check
bun run build

@@@FILE .jules/spec.md@@@
# Spec: Krishi Bazar v2

Architect-owned. Agents read it; they never edit it. User stories are `U<n>`, seams are `S<n>`. Task files cite both.

## Problem Statement
Bangladeshi farmers lose income to middlemen, and buyers cannot verify how fresh produce actually
is. The legacy prototype proved the concept but cannot go to production: it exposes every buyer's
phone and address on a public orders page, computes money with floating point, can oversell stock
under concurrent orders, stores data in SQLite and photos on a local disk (impossible on Vercel),
has no authentication, no validation, no tests, no CI, Bangla-only text, one unit (kg), and a
naive freshness badge. The owner wants a competition-grade, professional, clean rebuild with no
bloat.

## Solution
A bilingual (Bangla default, English switchable) mobile-first marketplace where farmers register
with a Bangladeshi mobile number and password, list harvests under a comprehensive
Bangladesh-specific taxonomy with grade, organic claim, unit, photos and harvest date, and receive
an advisory Fair price they can accept or override. Buyers browse, search, filter and order as
guests; stock is reserved atomically; payment is cash-on-delivery with direct phone contact.
Farmers process orders through a strict lifecycle with automatic expiry. Comments carry a
Verified-buyer badge and one-level farmer replies, rate-limited per IP. Freshness is computed from
real-world per-category shelf-life windows pinned to Asia/Dhaka. Deployed on Vercel (Bun runtime),
Neon Postgres free tier, Vercel Blob.

## User Stories
1. **U1**: As a Buyer, I want to browse available Listings with freshness, grade and organic badges, price with unit conversion, and farmer District, so that I can trust what I am buying at a glance.
2. **U2**: As a Buyer, I want to search by name and filter by Group/Category, Grade, Organic claim and District, sort by newest harvest or price, and page through results, so that I find what I need in a large marketplace.
3. **U3**: As a Buyer, I want a Listing detail page with all photos, the best-before date, stock, and the Farmer's contact, so that I can decide and negotiate directly.
4. **U4**: As a Buyer, I want to order any allowed quantity with a Delivery speed as a guest, see a live total estimate that matches the server-computed total exactly, and get a confirmation with the Farmer's phone, so that ordering is simple and honest.
5. **U5**: As a Buyer, I want to cancel my Order while it is still নতুন, so that I am not committed to a mistake.
6. **U6**: As a Buyer, I want to comment on a Listing with my name and optional phone, see Verified-buyer badges, and never see spam floods, so that I can judge produce from real purchasers.
7. **U7**: As a Farmer, I want to register with my Bangladeshi mobile number and a password, log in and log out, and know my dashboard is private, so that nobody can touch my Listings or Orders.
8. **U8**: As a Farmer, I want to create a Listing with Category, Unit, Stock, Grade, Organic claim, harvest date, District plus village, up to three WebP photos, and a price — seeing an advisory Fair price with its reason and applying it in one tap or setting my own freely — so that I price confidently without being forced.
9. **U9**: As a Farmer, I want to edit, pause, resume and delete my own Listings and see their stock and order counts, so that my storefront stays accurate.
10. **U10**: As a Farmer, I want to see only my received Orders with buyer contact details, accept, reject with a reason, mark পথে and সম্পন্ন, and have unaccepted Orders expire automatically after 24 hours with Stock released, so that I never oversell and buyers never wait on a dead order.
11. **U11**: As a Farmer, I want to reply once to any Comment on my Listings with a কৃষক badge, so that I can answer questions publicly.
12. **U12**: As any visitor, I want to switch Bangla/English instantly without reload, have my choice remembered, and see numbers and dates formatted for my Locale, so that the product feels native in both languages.
13. **U13**: As any visitor, I want dark and light themes following my system preference with a manual override, so that the site is comfortable day and night.
14. **U14**: As any visitor, I want fast, accessible, mobile-first pages with polished empty states, proper 404/500 pages and no layout jank, so that the product feels professional.
15. **U15**: As a developer, I want `bun install && bun run db:reset && bun run db:seed && bun run dev` to give me a fully working marketplace with season-realistic demo data and zero external services, so that onboarding and judging are frictionless.
16. **U16**: As the operator, I want to deploy to Vercel Hobby with Neon free Postgres and Vercel Blob by setting documented environment variables, so that production runs at zero cost.

## Implementation Decisions
Runtime and platform (ADR-0001): Bun is the single toolchain — package manager (`bun.lock`), dev,
tests, build; production runs on Vercel Functions with the Bun runtime (adapter `runtime` set to
the Bun value; `bunVersion` 1.4.x in `vercel.json`; Beta on Vercel — recorded as a risk). Code
under `src/` stays runtime-agnostic (web standards + `node:crypto`), so switching the adapter
runtime to `nodejs24.x` is a one-line fallback. Function region `bom1`. SvelteKit 3, Svelte 5,
TypeScript strict, `@sveltejs/adapter-vercel` 7 — exact versions only from `.jules/deps.csv`.

Money and quantity (ADR-0002): every money value is an integer number of Paisa; quantities are
decimals in the Listing's Unit (kg and maund 2 decimals, litre 2 decimals, count units integers).
Order totals — goods plus delivery surcharge — are computed only by one pure pricing function on
the server; the browser renders the same pure function for the live estimate. Display converts
Paisa to Taka per Locale (Bengali numerals in `bn`).

Execution model (ADR-0003): tasks run strictly sequentially, one Jules agent at a time.

Database: Neon Postgres (free tier) in production via the serverless HTTP driver; PGlite
(embedded WASM Postgres, officially Bun-compatible) for local dev and all automated tests, chosen
by one env var through a Drizzle adapter seam. Drizzle ORM with SQL migrations committed to the
repo. Tables: Farmer, Session, Listing, Order, Comment (self-referencing for the single farmer
reply level), IpEvent (rate-limit windows). Taxonomy, Units, Districts, seasons, shelf-life
windows, average piece weights and reference prices live in versioned code data modules — never
in the database — each cell editable and covered by tests. Stock is reserved with a single atomic
conditional UPDATE at Order creation and released at any terminal transition; an oversell race is
a named test.

মাণ decision: 1 মণ = **40 kg** — the live Bangladesh bazaar convention (Wikipedia records 40 kg
throughout Bangladesh; the historical metric maund is 37.3242 kg). The constant lives in one data
module, is documented in both units UI copy and code comment, and switching it is a one-cell edit.

Categories: two-level taxonomy — 9 Groups, ~100 Categories — as approved in the grill, each with
Bangla and English names, allowed Units, shelf-life window (prime/ok days), season months and
average piece weights where count-units apply.

Freshness: computed server-side from harvest date against the Category window, Asia/Dhaka pinned.
States: harvested today (special), prime (তাজা), ok (amber), past (red + check-before-order hint);
the detail page and cards show a best-before date = harvest + ok days.

Fair price: pure function over Category reference price (sourced from DAM daily market reports and
the AgriPriceBD dataset, recorded with provenance in the data module), season-month multipliers,
Grade and Organic-claim factors, Unit, and — once a Category has ≥5 completed Orders — a blend
toward the platform's own median. Returns min/fair/max in Paisa plus a one-line human reason in
both Locales. Advisory only: the Farmer's manual price always wins; no validation ties the price
to the advice.

Delivery: two speeds (express 1 day, standard 3 days) charging per-kg surcharges over the Order's
kg-equivalent mass (mass/litre units convert directly with densities; count units via Category
average weights). Rates live in one config module. No live courier integration.

Orders lifecycle: নতুন → গৃহীত → পথে → সম্পন্ন; terminal বাতিল via farmer rejection (reason enum:
stock-out, wrong price, other), buyer cancellation while নতুন, or 24-hour auto-expiry evaluated
lazily on every read (no cron). Every invalid transition is rejected. Buyer phone and address are
visible only to the owning Farmer and on the buyer's own confirmation; a Buyer cannot order with
the Farmer's own phone number. Cash-on-delivery only; no payment gateway; no notifications —
the dashboard and confirmation page are the channels.

Auth: Farmer accounts — normalized BD mobile (01[3-9]XXXXXXXX) + password hashed with scrypt
(`node:crypto`), opaque session tokens in the Session table, httpOnly SameSite=Lax secure cookies,
30-day absolute expiry. Open registration, no admin role, no password reset in v1 (accepted risk;
the session module is the documented seam for future OTP/reset). Guests never hold sessions.

Photos: 1–3 per Listing, WebP only. The browser converts and downscales (canvas → WebP, ≤1600px
long edge, quality ≈0.82, ≤2MB) with feature detection and a localized error for browsers that
cannot encode WebP; upload goes direct to Vercel Blob via the handleUpload token flow (the token
route is auth-gated and IP-rate-limited). The server re-validates type and size at token issue;
displayed images degrade to a local placeholder on decode failure. No hotlinked stock images.

Comments: guests post name + optional phone; DB-backed sliding-window rate limit per IP (3/hour,
10/day) using IpEvent, honeypot field, length caps, newest first; Verified-buyer badge computed at
read time when the phone matches a non-cancelled Order on the same Listing; the owning Farmer may
post exactly one reply per Comment, badged কৃষক.

i18n: typed message dictionaries for `bn` (default) and `en` as per-module drop-in files; instant
cookie-backed toggle, no reload, no URL prefixes; `Intl` number/date formatting per Locale;
`<html lang>` updates; CI test asserts key parity across Locales. Farmer-entered content is never
translated.

Design system: hand-written CSS only — a token layer (OKLCH palette grown from the brand emerald
and amber, type scale, spacing, radii, elevation, motion), a base/reset layer, then scoped
component styles; dark theme by token swap driven by `prefers-color-scheme` plus a manual toggle
persisted in a cookie; self-hosted Hind Siliguri (bn) and Inter (en); `@lucide/svelte` icons;
mobile-first; AA contrast, visible focus, reduced-motion respected. No CSS framework, no CDNs, no
third-party runtime requests.

Marketplace scale: keyset (cursor) pagination on (harvest date, id), 24 per page; ILIKE name
search; filters for Group/Category, Grade, Organic, District (structured 64-district dropdown +
free-text village); sorts: newest harvest (default), price ascending, price descending. Only
active Listings with stock are shown; paused, deleted and zero-stock Listings are hidden.

Validation: one zod schema set shared by client and server; every form action re-validates
server-side and returns field-level errors in the active Locale. Bounds: price ১–১,০০,০০০ ৳ per
unit; stock 0.5–10,000 mass/litre or 1–10,000 count; harvest date ≤ today and not older than
twice the Category ok-window; name ≤120 chars; description ≤1000; address ≤500; comment ≤600.

Security: security headers (CSP, X-Content-Type-Options, Referrer-Policy, X-Frame-Options,
Permissions-Policy) applied in one server hook; parameterized SQL everywhere via Drizzle;
rate limits on auth, order, comment and upload-token endpoints; secrets only via env vars.

Ops: `/api/health` returns JSON status; custom bilingual 404/500 pages; dev-only season-aware seed
(current month drives which Categories appear — ইলিশ peaks in আশ্বিন, আম is absent, খেজুর গুড়
arrives in পৌষ); competition-grade bilingual README with screenshots, architecture overview,
setup and deployment guides; `robots.txt`; OG/meta per Listing; MIT license.

## Testing Decisions
What a good test is here: external behaviour observed through a public interface — a domain
function's output, an HTTP-level request/response plus resulting database state, or a browser
journey. Never implementation details; never component internals.

Seams (highest possible; agents test only here):
- **S1**: Domain modules — every pure rule: Paisa arithmetic and rounding, Unit conversions and
  kg-equivalents, Freshness states and boundaries per Category, Fair-price outputs and reasons,
  the Order state machine (every legal and illegal transition), validation schemas, rate-limit
  windows, Verified-buyer matching, Locale formatting and dictionary parity. Includes
  property-based tests: money never drifts more than one Paisa, stock is conserved across random
  order/cancel/expire sequences, fair price is monotonic in Grade and bounded by min/max.
- **S2**: Server surface — every form action and API endpoint driven in-process against a
  disposable PGlite database: auth registration/login/logout and session expiry, farmer-only
  authorizations, every validation denial, order creation with atomic reservation, the parallel
  oversell race (two concurrent orders, last one fails), lazy expiry recomputation and restock,
  comment rate limiting and badge computation, upload token issuance rules, listing
  pause/delete/edit ownership.
- **S3**: Browser E2E (Playwright + axe-core) against the built app on PGlite: register → login →
  create listing with photo → appears in marketplace → buyer searches/filters → orders with speed
  and manual price context → confirmation shows farmer phone → farmer accepts → পথে → সম্পন্ন;
  reject-with-reason restocks; buyer cancels a নতুন order; comment + verified badge + farmer
  reply; language toggle persists and reformats numbers; dark mode toggle; 404 page; axe
  violations zero on key pages; one pass at 360px width.

Modules tested: all domain modules (gate: ≥95% statements and branches), all route server logic,
overall coverage ≥85% — enforced in CI. Prior art in the repo: none (greenfield); the foundation
task establishes the S1/S2/S3 harness patterns every later task copies.

## Out of Scope
Online payment and payment gateways; SMS/email/push notifications; password reset and account
recovery (seam documented); admin or moderation dashboards; buyer accounts and buyer order
history; URL-prefixed locales and machine translation of farmer content; partial order
fulfillment; region-specific price factors (seam documented); organic certification verification;
native mobile apps; multi-currency; courier tracking integrations; listing auto-archiving.

## Further Notes
Competition context: judges clone the repo and click the deployed URL — README quality, seed-data
realism, Lighthouse budgets (Perf ≥90, A11y ≥95, Best-Practices ≥95, SEO ≥95) and the bilingual
toggle are first-class deliverables, not afterthoughts.
Reference-price provenance: DAM daily market reports (market.dam.gov.bd) and the AgriPriceBD
public dataset; where no published retail figure exists for a Category, the seed value is a
documented estimate — every cell of the reference table is owner-editable without touching code.
Neon free tier scales to zero after 5 minutes: the first request after idle pays a cold start —
accepted for v1.
Vercel Blob Hobby-plan quota: verify remaining storage in the dashboard before the demo.
All user-facing strings must come from the dictionaries; hardcoded text fails review.
Glossary terms are binding in code identifiers, test names, task files and UI copy.

# T01: Scaffold toolchain CI and tracer skeleton

Wave: 0 | Model: default | Size: L | Mode: foundation
Branch: `jules/T01-scaffold-toolchain` | PR title: `[T01] Scaffold toolchain CI and tracer skeleton`
Skills: domain-language, tdd, codebase-design, code-review, pr
Serves: infrastructure (enables U14, U16) | Seams under test: S3

Read `/AGENTS.md` first. It overrides anything below that conflicts with sections 5, 6, 8, 11.

## Objective
A fresh clone becomes a working, verified SvelteKit 3 app on Bun with one command. After this task: `bun install --frozen-lockfile && bun run verify` is green, `/api/health` answers JSON, the pre-shipped CI workflow matches the package scripts exactly, and the whole plan's dependency set is installed at the versions pinned in `.jules/deps.csv`.

## Context pointers
- Always: `GLOSSARY.md`; `.jules/spec.md` (Testing Decisions, Further Notes); `.jules/deps.csv` (every version you install).
- ADRs: `docs/adr/0001-bun-runtime.md` (runtime-agnostic rule), `0002-money-integer-paisa.md`, `0003-sequential-execution.md`, `0004-neon-pglite-seam.md`.
- Pre-shipped files you must not rewrite, only align with: `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `vercel.json`, `.env.example`, `src/lib/data/taxonomy.ts`, `src/lib/data/districts.ts`.

## Owned paths (write)
package.json, bun.lock, tsconfig.json, svelte.config.js, vite.config.ts, vitest.config.ts, playwright.config.ts, eslint.config.js, .prettierrc.json, .prettierignore, commitlint.config.js, .gitignore, .env.example, vercel.json, .github/workflows/**, src/app.html, src/app.d.ts, src/hooks.server.ts, src/routes/api/health/**, src/routes/+page.svelte, src/lib/test/**, static/**, scripts/lighthouse/**, e2e/smoke.spec.ts
Also yours: `.jules/reports/T01.md`

## Read-only inputs
`.jules/deps.csv`, `src/lib/data/*.ts`, `AGENTS.md`, `GLOSSARY.md`, `.jules/spec.md`

## Forbidden
- Any path not listed under Owned paths.
- New dependencies or version changes beyond `.jules/deps.csv` — you install exactly that ledger (you are the only task allowed to).
- Weakening any CI job that ships pre-written.

## Steps
1. Scaffold SvelteKit 3 (Svelte 5, TS strict) with Bun. `package.json`: `"engines": {"bun": ">=1.4.3"}`; scripts exactly: `dev`, `build`, `preview`, `check` (svelte-check --tsconfig ./tsconfig.json), `lint` (eslint .), `format` (prettier --check .), `test:unit` (vitest run --coverage), `test:e2e` (playwright test), `verify` (check && lint && format && test:unit && test:e2e && build), `db:generate` (drizzle-kit generate), `db:migrate`, `db:reset`, `db:seed` (last three point at scripts/db/* files that T03/T17 create — define the script entries now; until the files exist they print a clear not-yet-available message and exit 1), `lh` (bun scripts/lighthouse/check.ts). Install every deps.csv npm row at its exact version (runtime deps vs devDeps by role). Commit `bun.lock`. Done when: `bun install --frozen-lockfile` succeeds from clean.
2. Wiring per pinned versions: vite.config.ts uses the SvelteKit 3 plugin form with adapter-vercel 7 (`runtime: 'bun1.x'`, `regions: ['bom1']`) — follow the adapter-vercel 7.0.0 docs for exact placement. tsconfig strict + `moduleResolution: bundler` per SvelteKit defaults. Done when: `bun run build` produces `.vercel/output`.
3. Tooling configs: eslint flat config (js recommended, typescript-eslint recommended, eslint-plugin-svelte flat/recommended + flat/prettier, eslint-config-prettier last); prettier with prettier-plugin-svelte; commitlint extends @commitlint/config-conventional; .gitignore includes `.data/`, `.lighthouse/`, `node_modules`, `.svelte-kit`, `build`, `coverage`, `test-results`, `playwright-report`, `.env`. Done when: `bun run lint && bun run format` pass on the scaffold.
4. `src/app.html`: `<html lang="bn">`, meta charset/viewport, theme-color, an inline pre-paint script that reads the `kb_theme` cookie (`light|dark`) and sets `data-theme` on `<html>` before render (prevents theme flash; T02 owns the toggle that writes the cookie). Done when: reloading with cookie `kb_theme=dark` shows `data-theme="dark"` before paint.
5. `src/hooks.server.ts`: `handle` adds security headers to every response (Content-Security-Policy: `default-src 'self'; img-src 'self' data: blob: https://*.public.blob.vercel-storage.com; style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; script-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; frame-ancestors 'none'; base-uri 'self'` — the inline theme script needs `'unsafe-inline'` in script-src for now, T19 hardens with nonces; X-Content-Type-Options nosniff; Referrer-Policy strict-origin-when-cross-origin; X-Frame-Options DENY; Permissions-Policy `camera=(), microphone=(), geolocation=()`), reads the `kb_locale` cookie into `event.locals.locale` (default `'bn'`), and extends `App.Locals` in `src/app.d.ts` (`locale: 'bn' | 'en'`; `farmer?: { id: string; phone: string } | null` — T06 fills it). Done when: `curl -sI` on preview shows all five headers.
6. Tracer skeleton: `src/routes/api/health/+server.ts` GET → `Response.json({ status: 'ok' })`; `src/routes/+page.svelte` minimal brand page (renders "কৃষি বাজার" — T02 replaces it; keep it honest and styled with plain CSS, no placeholder copy like "coming soon"). `static/`: favicon.svg (simple wheat/green mark you draw as inline SVG), placeholder.webp (a small solid-color valid WebP used for listings without photos — generate it once with a throwaway script, commit the binary; do not commit the script outside owned paths). Done when: health returns JSON 200 in preview.
7. Test harness: vitest.config.ts (environment node, coverage provider v8, `coverage.include: ['src/lib/**']`, no thresholds yet — T04 adds them); playwright.config.ts (chromium project, `webServer: { command: 'bun run build && bun run preview --port 4173', reuseExistingServer: !process.env.CI }`, baseURL http://localhost:4173, trace on-first-retry); `src/lib/test/utils.ts` (tiny helpers later tasks reuse: `makeRequest` wrappers — keep minimal, only what smoke needs); `e2e/smoke.spec.ts` (Slice 1, S3, red first: home loads and shows the brand; `/api/health` JSON ok; security header present). Done when: `bun run verify` exits 0.
8. `scripts/lighthouse/check.ts`: spawns `bun run preview --port 4173`, runs the pinned `lighthouse` CLI (chrome flags headless) against `/` (mobile form factor), writes JSON to `.lighthouse/`, compares scores to `scripts/lighthouse/budgets.json` (`{"performance":90,"accessibility":95,"best-practices":95,"seo":95}`); **failing budgets only fail the process when the file `scripts/lighthouse/enforce` exists** (T19 creates it); without it, print the scores and exit 0. Done when: `bun run lh` prints scores and exits 0.
9. CI alignment: the pre-shipped `.github/workflows/ci.yml` references `bun run check/lint/format/test:unit/test:e2e/build/lh` and `bunx --bun commitlint` — make every referenced script exist with exactly that name. Do not restructure the workflow. Done when: every `run:` command in ci.yml resolves to a package script or committed file.
10. Self-review pass (skill `code-review`): Spec axis against this file and AGENTS.md sections 3–5, then Standards.

## Acceptance criteria
1. `bun install --frozen-lockfile && bun run verify` exits 0 on a fresh clone.
2. `bun run build` succeeds and produces Vercel output configured for the Bun runtime (`runtime: 'bun1.x'`, region `bom1` visible in build output config).
3. Smoke S3: `bunx playwright test e2e/smoke.spec.ts` green (home, health, header).
4. `curl -sI http://localhost:4173/ | grep -i content-security-policy` shows the CSP.
5. `bun run verify` passes and `python .jules/tools/slop_guard.py` reports no ERROR.

## Decision defaults
- Versions come only from `.jules/deps.csv`; if `bun install` resolves a transitive conflict, adjust the lockfile by reinstalling — never change a pinned direct version.
- Runtime-agnostic rule (ADR-0001): no `Bun.*` APIs in `src/**`; scripts/ may use them.
- Keep configuration files minimal and commented only where a choice is surprising.
- The brand mark is a simple inline SVG; no image dependencies, no external fonts yet (T02 wires fontsource).

## Fallbacks
- If adapter-vercel 7 rejects `runtime: 'bun1.x'` in its current docs shape, configure the closest documented equivalent (`vercel.json` `bunVersion: "1.4.x"` is already shipped) and record the deviation in the report.
- If Playwright browser download fails in your VM, retry once with `bunx playwright install chromium --with-deps`; if still failing, mark the e2e criterion `[PARTIAL]` and note it — unit-level criteria still must pass.

## Hand-off
Report at `.jules/reports/T01.md`. For T02: the theme cookie name is `kb_theme`, locale cookie `kb_locale`, `locals.locale` exists; `src/routes/+page.svelte` and `static/placeholder.webp` are yours to replace/consume. For T03: `db:*` script entries exist and expect `scripts/db/{migrate,reset,seed}.ts`. For T04: vitest coverage include is `src/lib/**` with no thresholds. For everyone: `bun run verify` is the gate.

# T02: Design system app shell and i18n core

Wave: 1 | Model: default | Size: M | Mode: foundation
Branch: `jules/T02-design-shell` | PR title: `[T02] Design system app shell and i18n core`
Skills: domain-language, tdd, codebase-design, code-review, pr
Serves: U12, U13, U14 | Seams under test: S3

Read `/AGENTS.md` first. It overrides anything below that conflicts with sections 5, 6, 8, 11.

## Objective
The product looks and feels like the competition-grade brand: a token-driven pure-CSS design system (emerald/amber, light+dark), the app shell (header with nav, language toggle, theme toggle; footer), the complete i18n core (typed bn/en dictionaries with drop-in auto-merge, cookie-backed instant switching, Intl formatters incl. Bengali numerals), localized 404/500 pages, and the home skeleton. After this task every later UI has tokens, primitives and two languages to build on.

## Context pointers
- Always: `GLOSSARY.md` (Locale, Freshness tones); `.jules/spec.md` stories U12–U14 and the Design-system + i18n decisions.
- From T01 (`.jules/reports/T01.md`): cookie names `kb_theme`/`kb_locale`, `locals.locale`, pre-paint theme script in `src/app.html`.
- When choosing colors: AA contrast is an acceptance criterion, not a goal.

## Owned paths (write)
src/styles/**, src/lib/i18n/**, src/lib/components/ui/**, src/lib/components/layout/**, src/routes/+layout.svelte, src/routes/+layout.ts, src/routes/+layout.server.ts, src/routes/+page.svelte, src/routes/+error.svelte, e2e/shell.spec.ts
Also yours: `.jules/reports/T02.md`

## Read-only inputs
src/app.html, src/hooks.server.ts, src/app.d.ts, src/lib/test/**, package.json (fontsource + lucide deps are installed)

## Forbidden
- Any path not listed above; `src/app.html` and `hooks.server.ts` stay as T01 shipped (requests via 5.7 if blocked).
- CSS frameworks, CDN links, raw hex colors in components (tokens only), new dependencies.

## Steps
1. `src/styles/tokens.css` (frozen contract after this task): CSS custom properties — OKLCH brand palette (emerald primary scale 50–900 from the legacy #2e7d32 family, amber accent scale), semantic tokens for light and dark (`[data-theme="dark"]` block): surface, surface-raised, text, text-muted, border, focus-ring, and status tones good/mid/old mapped to the Freshness badge states; 4px spacing scale; type scale tuned for Hind Siliguri; radii; elevation shadows; motion durations/easings; `prefers-reduced-motion` zeroing motion. `src/styles/base.css`: minimal modern reset, body defaults, `:focus-visible` ring, selection color. Import both plus `@fontsource/hind-siliguri` (400,600,700) and `@fontsource-variable/inter` in the root layout; font-family switches with locale (`bn` → Hind Siliguri first, `en` → Inter first). Done when: both themes render the skeleton with AA contrast (verify text/surface pairs ≥ 4.5:1 — compute, don't eyeball).
2. i18n core `src/lib/i18n/index.ts` (frozen contract): `type Dict` built from `import.meta.glob('./{bn,en}/*.ts', { eager: true })` auto-merge per locale; `t(key, params?)` with `{param}` interpolation, missing key → throws in dev/test, renders the key in prod; locale store hydrated from `locals.locale`; `setLocale(l)` writes cookie `kb_locale` (1y, path=/, SameSite=Lax) and updates the store + `document.documentElement.lang` instantly, no reload. Seed dictionaries `bn/common.ts` + `en/common.ts` (brand, nav, actions, theme labels, 404/500 copy, empty states). Done when: switching locale re-renders every common string and survives reload.
3. `src/lib/i18n/format.ts` (frozen contract): `formatTaka(paisa, locale)` → `৳১,২৩৪` (bn-Bengali numerals, lakh grouping via `Intl.NumberFormat('bn')`) / `৳1,234` (en); `formatNumber(n, locale)`; `formatQty(qtyHundredths, unitId, locale)` (trims trailing zeros); `formatDate(iso, locale)` (`Intl.DateTimeFormat`, bn → Bangla month names). All money display goes through `formatTaka` (ADR-0002). Done when: unit-tested inline via shell spec assertions in step 6.
4. UI primitives `src/lib/components/ui/`: Button (variants primary/secondary/danger/ghost, sizes, loading state), Field (label + input + error slot, wires aria-invalid/aria-describedby), Textarea, Select, Badge (tones: good/mid/old/brand/neutral), Card, EmptyState (icon slot + title + hint + action slot), Skeleton (shimmer respecting reduced-motion). Scoped CSS consuming tokens only. Done when: each primitive renders in both themes with visible focus states.
5. Layout shell: `src/lib/components/layout/` AppHeader (inline-SVG brand mark + wordmark from `common` dict, nav: marketplace `/`, farmer area `/farmer` — link only, pages come later tasks; LanguageToggle bn/en segmented control; ThemeToggle light/dark/system tri-state writing `kb_theme` cookie + `data-theme`), AppFooter (brand line, glossary-powered tagline, no dead links). `+layout.svelte` composes them; `+layout.server.ts` passes `locals.locale`. `+page.svelte`: hero (anti-middleman promise line from the legacy brand: দালালমুক্ত সরাসরি কৃষক-ক্রেতা প্ল্যাটফর্ম / "Direct farmer-to-buyer, no middlemen") + EmptyState for listings (T11 replaces the page body with the marketplace). `+error.svelte`: localized 404 and 500 states with a way home. Done when: shell renders bn/en, dark/light, 360px-wide without horizontal scroll.
6. `e2e/shell.spec.ts` (Slice, S3, red first per behaviour): home shows brand; language toggle switches nav text without reload; locale persists across navigation and full reload (cookie); `formatTaka` output visible somewhere on home in bn shows Bengali numerals (assert `৳` + a `[০-৯]` char); theme toggle sets `data-theme` and persists after reload; unknown URL shows localized 404 text in the active locale; axe-core: zero violations on home in both locales. Done when: `bunx playwright test e2e/shell.spec.ts` green.
7. Self-review pass (skill `code-review`): Spec then Standards; check every string lives in both dictionaries.

## Acceptance criteria
1. `bunx playwright test e2e/shell.spec.ts` green (toggle persistence, numerals, 404, axe).
2. No raw color literals outside `src/styles/tokens.css`: `grep -rEn "#[0-9a-fA-F]{3,8}|oklch\(" src/lib/components src/routes | grep -v tokens` prints nothing.
3. Every `t()` key used exists in both `bn/` and `en/` files (add a vitest parity check inside `src/lib/i18n/parity.test.ts` that globs both locales and diffs key sets — this test is the CI key-parity gate).
4. `bun run verify` passes and `python .jules/tools/slop_guard.py` reports no ERROR.

## Decision defaults
- Dictionary keys: `module.snake_case` (e.g. `common.nav_market`); modules add their own files (drop-in registry, AGENTS 5.6).
- Toggles write cookies with `path=/; SameSite=Lax; max-age=31536000`; theme `light|dark|system`.
- Icons: `@lucide/svelte` only; decorative icons get `aria-hidden`.
- Mobile-first: base styles target 360px; enhance upward.

## Fallbacks
- If `import.meta.glob` typing fights TS strict, type the glob result as `Record<string, { default: Dict }>` explicitly — keep auto-merge.
- If a fontsource import breaks the Vercel build, import the CSS files from `+layout.svelte` `<style>`-adjacent script instead of app.html and note it.

## Hand-off
Report at `.jules/reports/T02.md`. For all later UI tasks: primitives list + token names; `t()`/formatters are the only sanctioned text path; parity test will fail PRs with one-locale strings. For T11: `+page.svelte` hero/EmptyState structure is yours to replace.

# T03: Data layer schema and PGlite-Neon adapter

Wave: 2 | Model: default | Size: M | Mode: foundation
Branch: `jules/T03-data-layer` | PR title: `[T03] Data layer schema and PGlite-Neon adapter`
Skills: domain-language, tdd, codebase-design, code-review, pr
Serves: infrastructure (enables U15, U16) | Seams under test: S2

Read `/AGENTS.md` first. It overrides anything below that conflicts with sections 5, 6, 8, 11.

## Objective
The whole database exists as one frozen Drizzle contract, with a single adapter seam that runs on embedded PGlite when `DATABASE_URL` is unset (dev, tests, CI — zero external services) and on Neon Postgres serverless HTTP when it is set (production). Migrations are generated, committed, and applied automatically in PGlite mode.

## Context pointers
- Always: `GLOSSARY.md`; `.jules/spec.md` (Implementation Decisions: Database, Orders lifecycle, Comments, Auth) and `docs/adr/0004-neon-pglite-seam.md`.
- `src/lib/data/districts.ts` and `src/lib/data/taxonomy.ts` are shipped contracts — the schema references their ids as text, never as foreign keys.

## Owned paths (write)
src/lib/db/**, drizzle/**, drizzle.config.ts, scripts/db/**, src/lib/data/districts.ts
Also yours: `.jules/reports/T03.md`

## Read-only inputs
src/lib/data/taxonomy.ts, src/lib/test/**, package.json (drizzle + neon + pglite installed)

## Forbidden
- Any path not listed above. Editing `taxonomy.ts` (request via 5.7 if it blocks you).
- Postgres extensions or features PGlite lacks — both drivers must run the identical SQL.

## Steps
1. `src/lib/db/schema.ts` — the frozen contract. Drizzle pgTable definitions with pgEnum types, glossary-named columns:
   - Enums: `unit_enum` (kg, pc, hali, dozen, aati, jhuri, bosta, litre, maund); `grade_enum` (premium, grade_1, grade_2); `listing_status` (active, paused, deleted); `order_status` (new, accepted, in_transit, delivered, cancelled); `reject_reason` (stock_out, wrong_price, other, expired); `speed_enum` (express, standard).
   - `farmers`: id uuid pk (app-generated), phone text unique not null (normalized `01XXXXXXXXX`), password_hash text not null, created_at timestamptz not null default now.
   - `sessions`: id text pk (sha256 hex of the session token — raw tokens are never stored), farmer_id uuid not null references farmers on delete cascade, expires_at timestamptz not null, created_at timestamptz not null default now.
   - `listings`: id uuid pk, farmer_id uuid not null ref, category_id text not null, name text not null, unit unit_enum not null, qty numeric(12,2) not null check ≥ 0, price_paisa integer not null check > 0, grade grade_enum not null, organic boolean not null default false, harvest_date date not null, district_id text not null, village text not null, description text not null default '', photos text[] not null default '{}' (app-enforces ≤3), status listing_status not null default 'active', created_at timestamptz not null default now.
   - `orders`: id uuid pk, listing_id uuid not null ref, buyer_name text not null, buyer_phone text not null, address text not null, qty numeric(12,2) not null check > 0, speed speed_enum not null, goods_paisa integer not null, delivery_paisa integer not null, total_paisa integer not null check = goods+delivery is app-enforced, status order_status not null default 'new', reject_reason reject_reason, cancel_token text not null, expires_at timestamptz not null, created_at / updated_at timestamptz not null default now.
   - `comments`: id uuid pk, listing_id uuid not null ref, farmer_id uuid ref (set only on farmer replies), parent_id uuid ref comments (top-level null), author_name text not null, author_phone text (optional — powers Verified buyer), body text not null, ip_hash text not null, created_at timestamptz not null default now; **unique partial index on parent_id where parent_id is not null and farmer_id is not null** (one farmer reply per comment).
   - `ip_events`: id bigserial pk, ip_hash text not null, kind text not null (auth|order|comment|upload), created_at timestamptz not null default now; index (ip_hash, kind, created_at).
   - Indexes: listings (status, harvest_date desc), listings(category_id), listings(district_id), listings(farmer_id), orders(listing_id), orders(status, expires_at), orders(buyer_phone), comments(listing_id, created_at desc).
   Done when: `bun run db:generate` emits migration SQL covering every table/index above and `drizzle/` is committed.
2. `src/lib/db/index.ts` — the adapter seam: `getDb()` returns a Drizzle client; `process.env.DATABASE_URL` set → `drizzle-orm/neon-http` with `@neondatabase/serverless`; unset → `drizzle-orm/pglite` on `.data/pglite` running committed migrations automatically at first use (async init helper `initDb()`; document that PGlite mode needs one `await initDb()` before queries — expose `db` as a lazy proxy so call sites stay sync). `src/lib/db/testing.ts`: `withTestDb(fn)` — fresh in-memory/temp-dir PGlite per suite, migrated, torn down after. Done when: a round-trip insert/select works in PGlite mode via a test.
3. `scripts/db/migrate.ts` (requires DATABASE_URL; applies committed migrations to Neon via drizzle-orm/neon-http migrator; prints each applied migration), `scripts/db/reset.ts` (PGlite-only: deletes `.data/pglite` and re-initializes; refuses with a clear message when DATABASE_URL is set), `scripts/db/smoke.ts` (optional human-run Neon check: connect, `select 1`, print latency). Wire to the `db:*` package script entries T01 created (scripts live in your paths; package.json entries already point here — if a path differs, file a request rather than editing package.json). Done when: `bun run db:reset` then `bun run db:migrate` on PGlite (DATABASE_URL unset → migrate is a no-op message; reset+auto-init leaves a migrated DB).
4. Tests (Slice per behaviour, S2, red first, via `withTestDb`): migrations apply to a fresh DB; every table round-trips a row; farmers.phone unique violation surfaces; listings qty check rejects negative; orders total check constraint; comments single-farmer-reply unique partial index rejects a second reply; `getDb()` returns neon client shape when DATABASE_URL is set (inject a fake URL, assert driver type — no network call). Done when: `bun run test:unit -- src/lib/db` green.
5. Self-review pass (skill `code-review`): Spec (every spec schema decision present) then Standards (glossary names, no floats for money columns, timestamptz everywhere).

## Acceptance criteria
1. `bun run db:reset && bun run test:unit -- src/lib/db` exits 0 with zero external services.
2. `drizzle/` contains committed SQL migrations; `git diff --stat drizzle/` after a second `db:generate` is empty (schema and migrations in sync).
3. Story U15 verified by test `src/lib/db/adapter.test.ts` at seam S2 (PGlite mode auto-migrates).
4. `bun run verify` passes and `python .jules/tools/slop_guard.py` reports no ERROR.

## Decision defaults
- UUIDs generated app-side with `crypto.randomUUID()` (runtime-agnostic, ADR-0001).
- All money columns integer paisa; all quantities numeric(12,2) in the Listing's unit; all timestamps timestamptz (UTC storage; Dhaka conversion is domain code, T04).
- English enum values in the DB; Bangla labels are i18n dictionary entries, never stored.
- `photos` is a text array of storage URLs; no separate table.

## Fallbacks
- If PGlite's migrator path resolution misbehaves inside the Vite build, import migrations via `import.meta.glob('../drizzle/**/*.sql', { eager: true, query: '?raw' })` and apply them in filename order — same guarantee, note it in the report.
- If the neon-http import breaks `bun run test:unit` (it must not be loaded in PGlite mode), make the neon branch a dynamic `await import()` inside `getDb()`.

## Hand-off
Report at `.jules/reports/T03.md`. For T04 and all server tasks: import `db`/`initDb`/`withTestDb` from `$lib/db`; the schema is frozen — wrong column = request (5.7), not edit. For T17: `scripts/db/seed.ts` slot is yours; reset exists.
@@@END@@@

# T04: Market data modules and domain core

Wave: 3 | Model: default | Size: L | Mode: foundation
Branch: `jules/T04-domain-core` | PR title: `[T04] Market data modules and domain core`
Skills: domain-language, tdd, codebase-design, code-review, pr
Serves: infrastructure (enables U1, U4, U8, U10, U12) | Seams under test: S1

Read `/AGENTS.md` first. It overrides anything below that conflicts with sections 5, 6, 8, 11.

## Objective
Every business rule of Krishi Bazar exists as pure, exhaustively tested domain code before any feature touches it: paisa money, the 9-unit Bangladesh unit system with kg-equivalents (মণ = 40 kg), per-category freshness, the order state machine with 24h expiry, IP rate-limit windows, and all zod validation schemas — plus the frozen rates config. Coverage gates switch on here.

## Context pointers
- Always: `GLOSSARY.md`; `.jules/spec.md` (Money, Units, Freshness, Orders lifecycle, Validation, মণ decision); `docs/adr/0002-money-integer-paisa.md`.
- Contracts you consume and test, never edit except `taxonomy.ts`/`rates.ts` which you own: `src/lib/data/taxonomy.ts`, `src/lib/data/districts.ts`.

## Owned paths (write)
src/lib/domain/**, src/lib/data/taxonomy.ts, src/lib/data/rates.ts, vitest.config.ts
Also yours: `.jules/reports/T04.md`

## Read-only inputs
src/lib/data/districts.ts, src/lib/db/schema.ts (enum value names must match), src/lib/i18n/format.ts

## Forbidden
- Any path not listed above. I/O of any kind inside `src/lib/domain/**` except `clock.ts` reading the current time through its injected parameter defaults.
- Floats in any money path (ADR-0002); new dependencies.

## Steps
1. `src/lib/data/rates.ts` (frozen contract): delivery surcharges per kg in paisa (`express: 1500`, `standard: 500`) with bn/en label keys and promised days (1/3); rate-limit windows per kind (auth 5/hr; order 10/hr; comment 3/hr + 10/day; upload 20/hr); session lifetime 30 days; order expiry 24h; pagination 24; photo limits (max 3, max edge 1600, quality 0.82, max bytes 2_097_152, accepted source types jpeg/png/webp); listing validation bounds (price 100–10_000_000 paisa per unit; qty 50–1_000_000 hundredths for mass/litre, 1–10_000 units for counts; name ≤120; description ≤1000; village ≤120; address ≤500; comment body ≤600; harvest not older than 2× the category ok-window). Done when: every number above lives here and nowhere else.
2. `src/lib/domain/clock.ts`: `todayDhaka(now = new Date()): string` (YYYY-MM-DD via `Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone: 'Asia/Dhaka' })`), `daysSinceDhaka(iso, now)`, `nowUtc()`. All "today" logic in the product flows through this module. Slice 1 (U8, S1) red first: a UTC instant just before/after Dhaka midnight returns the correct Dhaka date. Done when: boundary tests green.
3. `src/lib/domain/money.ts`: quantities are **integer hundredths of a unit** (`qtyH`) everywhere in domain code. `takaStrToPaisa(s)` (half-up, rejects >2dp), `paisaToTakaStr(p, locale)` (delegates display to `format.ts`), `mulQty(pricePaisa, qtyH)` = roundHalfUp(price×qtyH/100), `addPaisa`, `kgUnitPricePaisa(pricePaisa, categoryId, unitId)` (per-kg display conversion via units). Slice (U4, S1) red first: `mulQty(4550, 150)` (৳45.50 × 1.5 kg) = 6825 exactly; half-up boundary 6824.5 → 6825. Done when: money tests green.
4. `src/lib/domain/units.ts`: `UNITS` meta (id, kind mass|volume|count, bn/en label keys, step in hundredths, min/max per rates); `kgEquivalentH(categoryId, unitId, qtyH)` using MAUND_KG=40, LITRE_KG density, category avgKg/bundleKg/basketKg/sackKg (count→kg rounds half-up to whole kg hundredths); `deliverySurchargePaisa(categoryId, unitId, qtyH, speed)` = mulQty(surchargePerKgPaisa, kgEquivalentH). Slice (U4, S1): 1 মণ চাল = 40 kg → express surcharge = 40×1500 = 60000 paisa; 1 হালি ডিম (4×0.06 kg) → surcharge rounds to 36 paisa. Done when: unit tests green incl. every unit kind.
5. `src/lib/domain/freshness.ts`: `freshnessOf(categoryId, harvestDateIso, today)` → `{ state: 'today'|'prime'|'ok'|'past', days, labelKey, bestBeforeIso }` where state = today if days 0; prime if ≤ primeDays; ok if ≤ okDays; else past; `bestBeforeIso` = harvest + okDays (Dhaka date math). Slice (U1, S1): leafy (1/3) harvested 1 day ago → prime; 3 days → ok; 4 → past; fish (0/1) harvested today → today state; harvested yesterday → ok; 2 days → past. Done when: boundary tests green for both windows of at least three categories incl. fish and grains.
6. `src/lib/domain/order-machine.ts`: states new/accepted/in_transit/delivered/cancelled; events accept, reject(reason), to_transit, deliver, buyer_cancel, expire; `transition(state, event)` → next state or `{ error: 'invalid_transition' }`; `restocksStock(event)` true for reject/buyer_cancel/expire; terminal = delivered|cancelled; `isExpired(expiresAtIso, now)`. Full 5×6 transition table asserted — every illegal pair returns the error. Done when: exhaustive table test green (30 cases).
7. `src/lib/domain/rate-limit.ts`: `hashIp(ip)` = sha256 hex of `kb:<ip>` (node:crypto — runtime-agnostic); `windowAllowed(eventTimestamps, limit, windowMs, now)` pure sliding-window check → `{ allowed, retryAfterSec }`; `limitsFor(kind)` from rates.ts. Slice (U6, S1): 3 comment events within the hour + 4th → denied with retryAfter ≤ 3600. Done when: window edge tests green (exactly-at-boundary timestamps).
8. `src/lib/domain/validate.ts`: zod 4.6 schemas sharing one error-shape `{ fieldErrors: Record<string, string-key> }`: `phoneSchema` (accepts `01XXXXXXXXX`, `+8801...`, `8801...`; normalizes to `01...`; rejects others), `registerSchema`, `loginSchema`, `listingSchema` (category exists in taxonomy; unit ∈ category.units; qty within per-kind bounds; price within bounds; harvest ≤ today and ≥ today − 2×okDays via clock; district exists; village/name/description lengths), `orderSchema` (qty > 0 and ≤ stock passed in; speed ∈ enum), `commentSchema` (name ≤80, body ≤600, optional phone normalized), `rejectSchema` (reason enum). Every failure carries a dictionary key + params, never prose. Done when: schema tests green incl. every rejection class with its key.
9. Taxonomy integrity tests (you own `taxonomy.ts` — fix data errors you find, record each fix in the report): ids unique kebab-case; every group non-empty; ≥90 categories; primeDays ≤ okDays; units ⊆ UNIT ids and default unit first; months values 1–12 sorted unique or null; avgKg required whenever 'pc' ∈ units; bundleKg/basketKg/sackKg present whenever আঁটি/ঝুড়ি/বস্তা ∈ units; refPricePaisa > 0; source ∈ {DAM-2026-10, estimate}; bn and en names non-empty. Done when: `bun run test:unit -- src/lib/data` green.
10. Property tests (fast-check, S1): taka→paisa→display round-trip stable for 0–2dp inputs; `mulQty` never drifts >1 paisa from exact rational; `kgEquivalentH` strictly increasing in qty for every unit kind; freshness state monotonic in days (never returns to a fresher state); transition() is deterministic and terminal states absorb every event. Done when: properties green with ≥200 runs each.
11. Coverage gates: in `vitest.config.ts` set thresholds for `src/lib/domain/**` to 95% statements and 95% branches (global include-scope gate comes in T19). Done when: `bun run test:unit` prints coverage and passes the gates.
12. Self-review pass (skill `code-review`): Spec then Standards — hunt any float that touched money, any `new Date()` outside clock.ts consumers' parameters.

## Acceptance criteria
1. `bun run test:unit -- src/lib/domain src/lib/data` green; coverage table shows `src/lib/domain` ≥95/95.
2. Story U4 arithmetic verified by `money.test.ts`+`units.test.ts` at S1; U10 transitions by `order-machine.test.ts`; U1 freshness by `freshness.test.ts`; U6 windows by `rate-limit.test.ts`; U8 validation by `validate.test.ts`.
3. `grep -rn "parseFloat\|Number(" src/lib/domain | grep -v test` shows money paths use only integer helpers (no float money arithmetic).
4. `bun run verify` passes and `python .jules/tools/slop_guard.py` reports no ERROR.

## Decision defaults
- Hundredths-integer quantities (`qtyH`) are THE domain quantity representation; DB numeric(12,2) maps 1:1 (string ↔ int hundredths helpers live in money.ts).
- Error messages are dictionary keys; domain never imports i18n dictionaries (keys flow out, translation happens in routes/components).
- Keep every module dependency-free of Svelte, DB and fetch — `tsc` should accept them in a bare Node/Bun context.

## Fallbacks
- If a fast-check property fails on a real edge (e.g. half-up vs banker's rounding ambiguity), fix the implementation to half-up per ADR-0002 and keep the property.
- If a taxonomy data error blocks a test, fix the cell in `taxonomy.ts` (you own it) and list every changed cell in the report.

## Hand-off
Report at `.jules/reports/T04.md`. For T08: `pricing.ts` is yours to author under these rules — it may import money/units/clock/data but nothing may import pricing except its consumers. For all feature tasks: validation = `domain/validate` schemas; time = `clock`; every rule you need likely exists here — read the module before writing logic.

# T06: Farmer auth — registration, login, sessions

Wave: 5 | Model: default | Size: M | Mode: feature
Branch: `jules/T06-farmer-auth` | PR title: `[T06] Farmer auth registration login sessions`
Skills: domain-language, tdd, code-review, pr
Serves: U7 | Seams under test: S1, S2

Read `/AGENTS.md` first. It overrides anything below that conflicts with sections 5, 6, 8, 11.

## Objective
A Farmer registers with a Bangladeshi mobile number and password, logs in and out, and every `/farmer/**` page is gated by a server-side session. Passwords are scrypt-hashed, sessions are opaque tokens stored hashed, cookies are httpOnly/SameSite=Lax/Secure, and auth endpoints are IP-rate-limited. Buyers remain guests — nothing here creates buyer identity.

## Context pointers
- Always: `GLOSSARY.md` (Farmer, Session); `.jules/spec.md` story U7 + Auth decisions.
- `.jules/reports/T05.md`: findings that touch auth or hooks — honor them.
- Contracts you consume: `src/lib/domain/validate.ts` (register/login/phone schemas), `src/lib/domain/rate-limit.ts` (auth window 5/hr), `src/lib/db/schema.ts` (farmers, sessions), `src/lib/data/rates.ts` (session lifetime).

## Owned paths (write)
src/lib/server/auth/**, src/lib/i18n/bn/auth.ts, src/lib/i18n/en/auth.ts, src/routes/farmer/+layout.server.ts, src/routes/farmer/+page.svelte, src/routes/farmer/register/**, src/routes/farmer/login/**, src/routes/farmer/logout/**, src/lib/components/auth/**, src/hooks.server.ts, e2e/auth.spec.ts
Also yours: `.jules/reports/T06.md`

## Read-only inputs
src/lib/domain/**, src/lib/db/**, src/lib/i18n/index.ts + format.ts, src/lib/components/ui/**, src/app.d.ts

## Forbidden
- Any path not listed above. `src/app.d.ts` already declares `locals.farmer` — fill it from hooks, don't re-declare.
- Storing raw tokens or plaintext/scrypt-less hashes; client-side-only gating; new dependencies.

## Steps
1. `src/lib/server/auth/password.ts` (Slice, U7, S1, red first): `hashPassword(pw)` → `scrypt$16384$8$1$<saltB64>$<hashB64>` via `node:crypto` scryptSync (16-byte random salt, keylen 64); `verifyPassword(pw, stored)` parses the params from the stored string and uses `timingSafeEqual`. Tests: round-trip, wrong password, tampered format rejected, two hashes of one password differ (salt).
2. `src/lib/server/auth/phone.ts` (S1): `normalizePhone(input)` per `domain/validate` phoneSchema → `01XXXXXXXXX` or throws the schema's keyed error. Tests: `+880`, `880`, `01` prefixes, rejects `012…`, rejects 9/12-digit.
3. `src/lib/server/auth/session.ts` (S1+S2): `createSession(db, farmerId)` → 32-byte `crypto.randomBytes` base64url token, stores sha256 hex with 30-day expiry (rates.ts), returns token; `readSession(db, token)` → farmer row or null, treats expired as null and deletes it; `destroySession(db, token)`. Cookie helper: `sessionCookie(token)` httpOnly, SameSite=Lax, Secure in production (`import { dev } from '$app/environment'`), path=/, max-age = lifetime. Tests via `withTestDb`: create→read round-trip, expiry enforced (insert backdated), destroy invalidates, raw token never appears in the sessions table (assert stored id = sha256 hex).
4. `src/hooks.server.ts` deepening: after T01's headers/locale, resolve `locals.farmer` from the `kb_session` cookie via `readSession` (single query; null when absent). Keep hooks thin — all logic stays in `lib/server/auth`. Done when: an authenticated request sees `locals.farmer` (prove in an S2 test through the real `handle`).
5. Registration (Slice, U7, S2, red first): `/farmer/register` page (Field primitives, phone + password + confirm, localized errors, link to login) and `+page.server.ts` action: rate-limit kind `auth` (5/hr/IP via `rate-limit.ts` + ip_events), validate with registerSchema, normalize phone, reject duplicate with keyed field error on phone, hash, insert farmer, create session, set cookie, redirect `/farmer`. Tests: happy path sets cookie + row exists; duplicate phone → fieldErrors.phone; 6th attempt in an hour → keyed rate-limit error; weak inputs rejected per schema.
6. Login + logout (Slice, U7, S2): `/farmer/login` with `returnTo` param (must start with `/` and not `//` — else fall back to `/farmer`; open-redirect test), same rate limit (kind `auth`), verifyPassword, session cookie, redirect returnTo. `/farmer/logout` POST-only action destroys session and clears the cookie. Tests: wrong password → keyed error and no session row; returnTo honored; `//evil.com` rejected; logout clears.
7. Gate: `src/routes/farmer/+layout.server.ts` — no `locals.farmer` → redirect(`/farmer/login?returnTo=<url.pathname>`). `src/routes/farmer/+page.svelte` — dashboard home: greeting with the Farmer's phone (formatted), member-since date (formatDate), logout button (POST), and a card list of the farmer area that later tasks fill (render links only to routes that exist now: none yet — show the localized "your tools appear as they ship" state ONLY IF no route exists; from T09 onward this page is deepened — keep the component structured for that with a `<slot>`-free explicit section list). Tests (S2): unauthenticated GET /farmer redirects to login; authenticated sees phone.
8. `e2e/auth.spec.ts` (Slice, U7, S3): register a new farmer through the UI → lands on /farmer with the phone visible; logout → /farmer now redirects to login; login with wrong password shows the localized error; login succeeds and returnTo lands back. Runs on PGlite with a fresh DB (webServer per playwright config).
9. Dictionaries `bn/en/auth.ts`: every string from steps 5–8 in both locales (parity test enforces).
10. Self-review pass (skill `code-review`): Spec then Standards — confirm no secrets in logs/reports, timing-safe compare used, cookie flags exact.

## Acceptance criteria
1. `bun run test:unit -- src/lib/server/auth` green (password, phone, session suites).
2. Story U7 verified by `src/lib/server/auth/actions.test.ts` at S2 (register/login/logout/guard/rate-limit) and by `e2e/auth.spec.ts` at S3.
3. `grep -rn "scryptSync\|timingSafeEqual" src/lib/server/auth` shows hashing only in password.ts.
4. `bun run verify` passes and `python .jules/tools/slop_guard.py` reports no ERROR.

## Decision defaults
- Password minimum 8 characters, maximum 200; no complexity rules (rural-UX decision recorded in the spec grill).
- Session token cookie name: `kb_session`. One session per login; multiple devices = multiple rows (allowed).
- Rate-limit denials return the same keyed-error shape as validation failures (fieldErrors with a form-level key), HTTP 200 with the form re-rendered (progressive enhancement), never a bare 429 HTML page.
- `ip_events.ip_hash` uses `domain/rate-limit.hashIp`.

## Fallbacks
- If reading the client IP is unreliable behind Vercel (`x-forwarded-for` first entry), use it when present, else `request.headers.get('x-real-ip')`, else `'local'` — record which path tests exercised.
- If the duplicate-phone race matters (two concurrent registers), rely on the DB unique constraint and map its error to the keyed field error.

## Hand-off
Report at `.jules/reports/T06.md`. For T07: `locals.farmer` is the upload gate; reuse `rate-limit` + `ip_events` with kind `upload`. For T09/T13/T16: the `/farmer` gate layout exists — nest authenticated pages under it. For T16: `/farmer/+page.svelte` dashboard is deepened by you later (listed in your owns).

# T07: Photo upload pipeline — WebP, direct-to-storage

Wave: 6 | Model: default | Size: M | Mode: feature
Branch: `jules/T07-photo-upload` | PR title: `[T07] Photo upload pipeline WebP direct-to-storage`
Skills: domain-language, tdd, code-review, pr
Serves: U8 | Seams under test: S1, S2

Read `/AGENTS.md` first. It overrides anything below that conflicts with sections 5, 6, 8, 11.

## Objective
Farmers attach 1–3 photos to a Listing. Every stored image is WebP ≤2MB, downscaled to ≤1600px in the browser before upload, uploaded **direct to Vercel Blob** (never through a function body — 4.5MB limit) via the handleUpload token flow, with a **local-disk storage adapter** used automatically when `BLOB_READ_WRITE_TOKEN` is unset (dev, tests, CI). The token endpoint is farmer-gated and IP-rate-limited.

## Context pointers
- Always: `GLOSSARY.md` (Listing); `.jules/spec.md` story U8 + Photos decisions.
- `.jules/reports/T06.md`: session gate + rate-limit patterns you reuse (kind `upload`, 20/hr from rates.ts).
- Pinned APIs: `@vercel/blob` 2.8.1 — use its documented client direct-upload (`@vercel/blob/client` `upload` with `clientToken`) and `handleUpload` callback shape from the 2.8.x docs, not memory.

## Owned paths (write)
src/routes/api/upload/**, src/lib/server/upload/**, src/lib/photos/**, src/lib/components/photos/**, src/lib/i18n/bn/photos.ts, src/lib/i18n/en/photos.ts
Also yours: `.jules/reports/T07.md`

## Read-only inputs
src/lib/server/auth/**, src/lib/domain/rate-limit.ts, src/lib/data/rates.ts (photo limits), src/lib/components/ui/**

## Forbidden
- Any path not listed above. Routing image bytes through a serverless function body in blob mode. New dependencies (no image libraries — canvas + Blob APIs only).

## Steps
1. `src/lib/photos/convert.ts` — pure helpers (Slice, U8, S1, red first): `planResize(width, height, maxEdge=1600)` → target dims (aspect kept, no upscaling); `isSupportedSource(type, sizeBytes)` against rates.ts photo limits; `webpEncodeSupported()` feature-detect wrapper around a probe (canvas `toBlob('image/webp')` result type check) designed for injection in tests; `conversionErrorCode` union for localized failures (`unsupported`, `too_large`, `decode_failed`, `encode_failed`). Tests: resize math (portrait/landscape/small-no-op), source acceptance matrix, every error code reachable.
2. `src/lib/photos/convert.browser.ts`: the DOM side — file → `createImageBitmap` → OffscreenCanvas/canvas → `toBlob('image/webp', 0.82)` with one quality-retry at 0.7 if the result exceeds 2MB, mapping failures to the error codes. No Svelte imports (usable from the component and testable in e2e only — keep logic in step 1's pure core).
3. `src/lib/server/upload/storage.ts` — the storage seam: `storageMode()` = `'blob'` when `BLOB_READ_WRITE_TOKEN` set else `'local'`; `issueToken(filename, contentType, sizeBytes)` → blob mode: `handleUpload`-style client token via `@vercel/blob/client` `generateClientTokenFromReadWriteToken`-equivalent from the 2.8.x API (follow pinned docs); local mode: signed-ish random id + record in `.data/uploads/index.json`; `localUploadPath(id)`; `publicUrlFor(id|blobUrl)`. Local files live under `.data/uploads/` (gitignored since T01) and are served by a route below. Done when: both modes round-trip a byte string in an S2 test (inject env per test).
4. `POST /api/upload/token` (Slice, U8, S2, red first): requires `locals.farmer` (401 JSON `{error,code}` otherwise — API routes use JSON per AGENTS conventions); rate-limit kind `upload` (20/hr/IP) → 429 JSON; body `{ filename, contentType, sizeBytes }` validated (contentType must be an accepted source type; sizeBytes ≤ 8MB pre-conversion, ≤ 2MB when contentType is already webp); returns `{ mode, clientToken?, uploadUrl? }` per storage mode. Tests: 401, 429 on 21st, bad type 400, oversize 400, blob-mode token shape (mock env), local-mode payload.
5. Local-mode file routes: `POST /api/upload/local` (multipart via `request.formData()`, farmer-gated, writes `.data/uploads/<id>.webp` after re-checking magic bytes `RIFF....WEBP` and size ≤2MB) and `GET /uploads-local/[name]` streaming the file with `Content-Type: image/webp` + immutable cache header (name validated against the index — no path traversal; traversal test required). Blob mode: `POST /api/upload/complete` implementing the `handleUpload` callback contract from the pinned `@vercel/blob` docs (validates token, returns `{ url }` JSON). Tests: magic-byte rejection (PNG bytes renamed .webp → 400), traversal `..%2f` → 404, happy round-trip.
6. `src/lib/components/photos/PhotoPicker.svelte` (client): props `max=3`, `name='photos'`; on file select runs convert.browser → immediate upload (blob or local mode from the token response) → shows thumbnail preview, progress state, per-file error with localized retry; hidden inputs carry the final public URLs in order; remove button drops a selection (orphaned blobs are acceptable — noted in spec Further Notes). Keyboard accessible, works at 360px. Done when: manual dev flow uploads 3 photos and the form serializes their URLs (proven in T09's e2e — here, a dev-mode harness page is NOT allowed; verify via component render in the T09 e2e and keep this task's proof at S1/S2).
7. Dictionaries `bn/en/photos.ts` (errors, button labels, limits copy). Parity test enforces both.
8. Self-review pass (skill `code-review`): Spec then Standards — magic-byte check server-side in BOTH modes, no secret leakage in tokens/logs.

## Acceptance criteria
1. `bun run test:unit -- src/lib/photos src/lib/server/upload` green; `bun run test:unit -- src/routes/api/upload` green.
2. Story U8 (photo pipeline portion) verified at S2 by `src/routes/api/upload/token.test.ts` and `local.test.ts` (auth, rate, validation, magic bytes, traversal, round-trip).
3. With `BLOB_READ_WRITE_TOKEN` unset, the full local flow works end-to-end in dev (demonstrated by the S2 round-trip test, not a manual claim).
4. `bun run verify` passes and `python .jules/tools/slop_guard.py` reports no ERROR.

## Decision defaults
- Accepted source types (pre-conversion): jpeg, png, webp. Output is always webp.
- Filenames in storage are random ids (`crypto.randomUUID()`); original names are never stored or logged.
- JSON error shape on all `/api/**`: `{ error: '<key>', code: <http status> }`.
- Pre-conversion size cap 8MB (phones rarely exceed); post-conversion hard cap 2MB.

## Fallbacks
- If the pinned `@vercel/blob` client-token API differs from the shape above, follow the installed package's docs exactly and record the call signatures in the report — the seam (`storage.ts`) keeps the rest untouched.
- If `createImageBitmap` is unavailable in a target browser, fall back to `HTMLImageElement` decoding inside convert.browser (same error codes).

## Hand-off
Report at `.jules/reports/T07.md`. For T09: drop `<PhotoPicker name="photos" max={3}/>` into the listing form; the action receives `photos: string[]` of public URLs (≤3, may be empty). For T11/T12: render `photo ?? '/placeholder.webp'` (T01's asset) with width/height attrs.
